Implement the following plan:

# Austria Map Feature — Bundesland Statistics

## Context
The ReportsPage already shows per-Bundesland statistics (participants, reports) in tables and charts. Adding a choropleth map of Austria colored by Bundesland gives an immediate visual overview — which regions are active, which are missing.

## Approach
Create an inline SVG component `AustriaMap` with the 9 Bundesländer shapes. No external map library needed — the shapes are static and simple. Color intensity based on participant count per Bundesland. Place it in ReportsPage between the summary cards and the charts.

## Changes

### 1. New component: `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`
- Inline SVG with path data for all 9 Austrian Bundesländer (OE1–OE9)
- Props: `data: { code: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]`
- Color scale: white (0 participants) → light blue → dark blue (`#1e3a5f`) based on max value
- Each Bundesland shape shows:
  - Fill color based on participant count
  - Label overlay: OE code + participant count (e.g. "OE8: 12")
  - Hover tooltip with full details (Bundesland name, participants, reports)
- Responsive: scales to container width via `viewBox`
- Supports PNG export (works with existing `html-to-image` setup)

### 2. Integrate into `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`
- Import `AustriaMap` component
- Add map card between summary cards (line ~226) and charts section (line ~228)
- Pass `blStats` data to the map component
- Add ref + download button for PNG export (same pattern as existing charts)
- Full-width card with title "Teilnehmer nach Bundesland"
- Include color legend (gradient bar with min/max labels)

## SVG Path Strategy
Use simplified SVG paths for Austria's Bundesländer. The paths represent approximate geographic shapes positioned correctly relative to each other within a ~800×500 viewBox. Each path has an `id` matching the bundesland code ("01"–"09") for data binding.

## Color Scale
```
0 participants  → #e8eef5 (very light blue-gray)
max participants → #1e3a5f (dark navy — matches brand)
```
Linear interpolation between the two. Bundesländer with 0 data get a light gray (#f3f4f6) to distinguish "no data" from "low count".

## Files Modified
- `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` (new)
- `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (add map section)

## Verification
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` compiles
2. Open ReportsPage for an exercise with data — map shows colored Bundesländer
3. Hover shows tooltip with details
4. "Download Auswertung" PNG export includes the map
5. Mobile responsive — map scales down properly
6. Deploy

    97→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
    98→    if (!cardRef.current || !exercise) return;
    99→    setExporting(true);
   100→    const header = createExportHeader();
   101→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   102→    // Wrap the card temporarily
   103→    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
   104→    wrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:24px;background:#f3f4f6;';
   105→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;
   106→    const next = cardRef.current.nextSibling;
   107→    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cardRef.current);
   108→    wrapper.appendChild(header);
   109→    wrapper.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   110→    wrapper.appendChild(footer);

   115→      link.href = url;
   116→      link.click();
   117→    } catch {} finally {
   118→      // Move card back to original position
   119→      if (next) parent.insertBefore(cardRef.current, next); else parent.appendChild(cardRef.current);

   422→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   423→                <thead>
   424→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   425→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 w-8">#</th>
   426→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Rufzeichen</th>
   427→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Name</th>
   428→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>
   429→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden md:table-cell">Bundesland</th>
   430→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   431→                  </tr>
   432→                </thead>
   433→                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   434→                  {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   435→                    <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   436→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   437→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}</td>
   438→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   439→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   440→                        {p.bezirk_code && (
   441→                          <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   442→                        )}
   443→                      </td>
   444→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   445→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   446→                    </tr>
   447→                  ))}
   448→                </tbody>
   449→              </table>
   450→              </div>
   451→            )}
   452→          </div>
   453→
   454→          {/* Export buttons */}
   455→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   456→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   457→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   458→              <button
   459→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   460→                disabled={exporting}
   461→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   462→              >
   463→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'PNG — Download Auswertung'}
   464→              </button>
   465→              {exercise.oe_link_enabled !== 0 && (
   466→                <a
   467→                  href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}
   468→                  className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   469→                  target="_blank"
   470→                >
   471→                  TXT — OE-Link
   472→                </a>
   473→              )}
   474→              <a
   475→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}
   476→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   477→                target="_blank"
   478→              >
   479→                TXT — Frequenzen
   480→              </a>
   481→              <a
   482→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/combined`}
   483→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   484→                target="_blank"
   485→              >
   486→                TXT — Kombiniert
   487→              </a>
   488→            </div>
   489→          </div>
   490→        </>
   491→      )}
   492→    </div>
   493→  );
   494→}

labelY: 325,\n  },\n};\n\nfunction getColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  // Interpolate between light (#c2d5ec) and dark (#1e3a5f)\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n}\n\nexport default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {\n  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{\n    x: number;\n    y: number;\n    code: string;\n    name: string;\n    participants: number;\n    reports: number;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));\n  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      <svg\n        viewBox=\"0 700 120 500\"\n        className=\"w-full h-auto\"\n        role=\"img\"\n        aria-label=\"Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland\"\n      >\n        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {\n          const bl = dataMap.get(code);\n          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;\n          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;\n          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);\n          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';\n\n          return (\n            <g\n              key={code}\n              onMouseEnter={(e) => {\n                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');\n                if (!svg) return;\n                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();\n                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();\n                pt.x = labelX;\n                pt.y = labelY;\n                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();\n                if (!ctm) return;\n                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);\n                setTooltip({\n                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,\n                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,\n                  code,\n                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,\n                  participants,\n                  reports,\n                });\n              }}\n              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}\n              className=\"cursor-pointer\"\n            >\n              <path\n                d={d}\n                fill={fill}\n                stroke=\"#ffffff\"\n                strokeWidth=\"2\"\n                className=\"transition-opacity hover:opacity-80\"\n              />\n              <text\n                x={labelX}\n                y={labelY - 6}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                fontSize=\"11\"\n                fontWeight=\"bold\"\n                fill={textColor}\n                pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              >\n                {code}\n              </text>\n              <text\n                x={labelX}\n                y={labelY + 8}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                fontSize=\"10\"\n                fill={textColor}\n                pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              >\n                {participants}\n              </text>\n            </g>\n          );\n        })}\n      </svg>\n\n      {/* Tooltip */}\n      {tooltip && (\n        <div\n          className=\"absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap\"\n          style={{\n            left: tooltip.x,\n            top: tooltip.y,\n            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',\n          }}\n        >\n          <div className=\"font-bold\">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>\n          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>\n          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Legend */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500\">\n        <span>0</span>\n        <div\n          className=\"h-3 w-32 rounded\"\n          style={{\n            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',\n          }}\n        />\n        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>\n        <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-400\">Teilnehmer</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

    83→
    84→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    85→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    86→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    87→  // Interpolate between light (#c2d5ec) and dark (#1e3a5f)
    88→  const t = value / max;
    89→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    90→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    91→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
    92→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    93→}
    94→
    95→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
    96→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    97→    x: number;
    98→    y: number;
    99→    code: string;
   100→    name: string;
   101→    participants: number;
   102→    reports: number;
   103→  } | null>(null);
   104→
   105→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   106→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   107→
   108→  return (
   109→    <div className="relative">
   110→      <svg
   111→        viewBox="15 75 700 310"
   112→        className="w-full h-auto"
   113→        role="img"
   114→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland"
   115→      >
   116→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   117→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   118→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   119→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   120→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   121→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   122→
   123→          return (
   124→            <g
   125→              key={code}
   126→              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
   127→                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   128→                if (!svg) return;
   129→                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   130→                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   131→                pt.x = labelX;
   132→                pt.y = labelY;
   133→                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   134→                if (!ctm) return;
   135→                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   136→                setTooltip({
   137→                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   138→                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   139→                  code,
   140→                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   141→                  participants,
   142→                  reports,
   143→                });
   144→              }}
   145→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   146→              className="cursor-pointer"
   147→            >
   148→              <path
   149→                d={d}
   150→                fill={fill}
   151→                stroke="#ffffff"
   152→                strokeWidth="2"
   153→                className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   154→              />
   155→              <text
   156→                x={labelX}
   157→                y={labelY - 6}
   158→                textAnchor="middle"
   159→                fontSize="11"
   160→                fontWeight="bold"
   161→                fill={textColor}
   162→                pointerEvents="none"
   163→              >
   164→                {code}
   165→              </text>
   166→              <text
   167→                x={labelX}
   168→                y={labelY + 8}
   169→                textAnchor="middle"
   170→                fontSize="10"
   171→                fill={textColor}
   172→                pointerEvents="none"
   173→              >
   174→                {participants}
   175→              </text>
   176→            </g>
   177→          );
   178→        })}
   179→      </svg>
   180→
   181→      {/* Tooltip */}
   182→      {tooltip && (
   183→        <div
   184→          className="absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
   185→          style={{
   186→            left: tooltip.x,
   187→            top: tooltip.y,
   188→            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',
   189→          }}
   190→        >
   191→          <div className="font-bold">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>
   192→          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>
   193→          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>
   194→        </div>
   195→      )}
   196→
   197→      {/* Legend */}
   198→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   199→        <span>0</span>
   200→        <div
   201→          className="h-3 w-32 rounded"
   202→          style={{
   203→            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',
   204→          }}
   205→        />
   206→        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>
   207→        <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400">Teilnehmer</span>
   208→      </div>
   209→    </div>
   210→  );
   211→}
   212→

Perfect! I have found all the information you need. Here's my detailed analysis:

## blStats Computation - Complete Analysis

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, lines 128-141

### SQL Query
```sql
SELECT
  COALESCE(bl.name, 'OE' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1), 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
  COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)) as bundesland_code,
  COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
  COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
FROM signal_reports sr
JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
  OR (o.bundesland_code IS NULL AND bl.code = '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
GROUP BY COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
ORDER BY bundesland_code
```

### bundesland_code Format

**The format is a TWO-DIGIT STRING: `"01"`, `"02"`, `"03"`, etc.**

As seen in the seed data (`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/seed.ts`, lines 14-33):
- `'01'` = OE1 Wien
- `'02'` = OE2 Salzburg
- `'03'` = OE3 Niederösterreich
- `'04'` = OE4 Burgenland
- `'05'` = OE5 Oberösterreich
- `'06'` = OE6 Steiermark
- `'07'` = OE7 Tirol
- `'08'` = OE8 Kärnten
- `'09'` = OE9 Vorarlberg
- Plus international codes: `'10'` (Slowenien), `'11'` (Tschechien), etc.

### How blStats Works

1. **Fallback Strategy**: The query uses a left join with a fallback mechanism:
   - If `o.bundesland_code` is populated in the operators table, it uses that
   - If `o.bundesland_code` is NULL, it derives the code from the operator's callsign by extracting character 3 and prepending `'0'` (e.g., callsign "OE1XYZ" → code "01")
   - The `bundesland_code` output column is: `COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))`

2. **Aggregation**: Groups by the derived/stored bundesland_code and counts:
   - `participants`: distinct operator_ids
   - `reports`: count of non-null readability values per bundesland

3. **Display Name**: The `bundesland` column shows the German name (e.g., "OE1 Wien") from the bundeslaender table, with fallback to a constructed name or "Sonstige" if not found.

Deployed. The issue was that the API returns `bundesland_code` as `"01"`, `"02"`, etc. but the map component keys were `"OE1"`, `"OE2"`. Fixed the mapping with `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}` to convert `"01"` → `"OE1"`.

     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→# Colors for output
     5→RED='\033[0;31m'
     6→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     7→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     8→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    12→    source .env.production
    13→else
    14→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    15→    echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→    exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→# Validate required variables
    20→REQUIRED_VARS="DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL JWT_SECRET"
    21→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    22→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    23→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    24→        exit 1
    25→    fi
    26→done
    27→
    28→# Check for --rebuild flag
    29→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    30→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    31→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    32→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}"
    33→fi
    34→
    35→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA Log to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}"
    36→echo "Host: $DEPLOY_HOST"
    37→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    38→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    39→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    40→echo ""
    41→
    42→# Step 1: Sync code to remote host
    43→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code...${NC}"
    44→GIT_URL=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    45→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    46→    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then
    47→        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull
    48→    else
    49→        mkdir -p '$(dirname $REMOTE_DIR)'
    50→        git clone '$GIT_URL' '$REMOTE_DIR'
    51→    fi
    52→"
    53→
    54→# Step 2: Copy .env for docker compose
    55→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Copying environment config...${NC}"
    56→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "cat > '$REMOTE_DIR/.env' << 'ENVEOF'
    57→JWT_SECRET=$JWT_SECRET
    58→ENVEOF"
    59→
    60→# Step 3: Build Docker image on remote
    61→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Building Docker image...${NC}"
    62→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    63→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    64→    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    65→"
    66→
    67→# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)
    68→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}"
    69→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    70→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    71→    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build
    72→"
    73→
    74→# Step 5: Verify deployment
    75→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 5: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    76→sleep 3
    77→
    78→# Check container status
    79→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    80→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    81→
    82→# Check local port
    83→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    84→if ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT/api/v1/auth/login"; then
    85→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    86→else
    87→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT — may still be starting${NC}"

    89→
    90→# Check public URL

    95→fi
    96→
    97→echo ""
    98→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"

Links: [{"title":"Free Blank Austria Map in SVG - Resources | Simplemaps.com","url":"https://simplemaps.com/svg/country/at"},{"title":"Download for free Austria vector map SVG | MapSVG","url":"https://mapsvg.com/maps/austria"},{"title":"Category:SVG maps of Austria - Wikimedia Commons","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Category:SVG_maps_of_Austria"},{"title":"GitHub - raphaellepuschitz/SVG-World-Map: 🗺 A JavaScript library to easily integrate one or more SVG world maps with all nations (countries) and second-level political subdivisions (countries, provinces, states).","url":"https://github.com/raphaellepuschitz/SVG-World-Map"},{"title":"File:Austria location map.svg - Wikimedia Commons","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Austria_location_map.svg"},{"title":"Free Blank Europe Map in SVG\" - Resources | Simplemaps.com","url":"https://simplemaps.com/resources/svg-europe"},{"title":"Free Blank World Map in SVG - Resources | Simplemaps.com","url":"https://simplemaps.com/resources/svg-world"},{"title":"File:Austria adm location map.svg - Wikipedia","url":"https://en.m.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Austria_adm_location_map.svg"},{"title":"Vector Maps of Austria | Free Vector Maps","url":"https://vemaps.com/austria"},{"title":"Map of Austria SVG Vector - Interactive HD Austria Map","url":"https://www.worldmaphd.com/austria/"}]

I found several resources for Austria SVG maps with Bundesländer (states) information. Here are the key findings:

## Main Resources:

**SimpleMaps (simplemaps.com)** - SimpleMaps has created multiple SVG maps for Austria that have been cleaned and optimized for web use. The maps are simplified to load quickly with minimal loss of detail (21.7 KB). First-level administrative regions are identified by name and id in the source code. The nine Bundesländer are mapped with IDs: AT1: Burgenland, AT2: Kärnten, AT3: Niederösterreich, AT4: Oberösterreich, AT5: Salzburg, AT6: Steiermark, AT7: Tirol, AT8: Vorarlberg, and AT9: Wien.

**MapSVG (mapsvg.com)** - Offers free blank SVG vector maps of Austria for any use, including commercial.

**Wikimedia Commons** - Contains Austria administrative location maps available as SVG files.

**Other Sources:**
- VEMaps provides editable Austria maps in multiple formats including SVG
- Various other mapping services offer Austria SVG resources

For inline SVG path data specifically, SimpleMaps appears to be the most straightforward option for getting clean, simplified paths with proper Bundesländer identification codes.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

# Austria SVG Map Resources

Based on the webpage content, MapSVG offers a **free downloadable SVG vector map of Austria**. 

The site states: "Download a blank vector map of Austria or make it interactive and insert it to your website."

**Download Link:** The page indicates you can access the map file at `/maps/geo-calibrated/austria.svg`

**Key Details:**
- The map is available for free download under Creative Commons BY 4.0 license
- "Any use, including commercial, is allowed"
- MapSVG also allows importing custom SVG files and converting them into interactive maps with clickable regions

However, the provided webpage content **does not include the actual SVG path data** for the 9 Austrian states (Bundesländer). To obtain the specific path coordinates, you would need to:

1. Download the SVG file directly from the link provided
2. Open it in a text editor to view the path data
3. Use MapSVG's interactive features to work with regional divisions

The page focuses on promoting MapSVG as a tool for making maps interactive rather than displaying raw SVG code.
```

Here are the SVG path data attributes for each Austrian state:

2. **Carinthia (AT-2)**: `M251.29,228.77L252.69,226.36L257.91,225.28...L255.37,231.9z`

3. **Lower Austria (AT-3)**: `M441.98,0.73l2.44,-0.07l0.3,1.92...L441.98,0.73zM546.93,88.21...L546.93,88.21z`

4. **Upper Austria (AT-4)**: `M347.18,30.34L349.28,30.66L355.86,37.17...L342.91,36.99z`

5. **Salzburg (AT-5)**: `M268.29,123.02L271.09,123.18L274.11,118.49...L269.44,127.15z`

6. **Styria (AT-6)**: `M418.42,152.69L420.64,155.27L424.91,152.95...L414.81,150.75z`

7. **Tyrol (AT-7)**: `M218.45,232.62l1.95,-0.42l2.26,-2.58...L219.22,157.07z`

8. **Vorarlberg (AT-8)**: `M24,170.85L25.87,172.6L25.05,176.35...L21.03,171.54z`

9. **Vienna (AT-9)**: `M550.84,84.85L551.18,84.93L551.18,84.93...L550.58,84.72z`

Each path element contains complete coordinate data for rendering that region's boundary polygon.

> AGENT

Perfekt — das sind die echten, geographisch korrekten Pfade. Die IDs sind `AT-1` bis `AT-9` und ich muss sie auf `OE1`–`OE9` mappen. Jetzt ersetze ich die handgezeichneten Pfade durch die echten.

Ich brauche auch die Label-Positionen (Mittelpunkte) für jedes Bundesland. Lass mich die berechnen und die Komponente komplett neu schreiben.

    84→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    85→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    86→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    87→  // Interpolate between light (#c2d5ec) and dark (#1e3a5f)
    88→  const t = value / max;
    89→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    90→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    91→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
    92→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    93→}
    94→
    95→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
    96→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    97→    x: number;
    98→    y: number;
    99→    code: string;
   100→    name: string;
   101→    participants: number;
   102→    reports: number;
   103→  } | null>(null);
   104→
   105→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   106→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   107→
   108→  return (
   109→    <div className="relative">
   110→      <svg
   111→        viewBox="15 75 700 310"
   112→        className="w-full h-auto"
   113→        role="img"
   114→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland"
   115→      >
   116→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   117→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   118→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   119→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   120→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   121→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   122→
   123→          return (
   124→            <g
   125→              key={code}
   126→              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
   127→                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   128→                if (!svg) return;
   129→                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   130→                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   131→                pt.x = labelX;
   132→                pt.y = labelY;
   133→                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   134→                if (!ctm) return;
   135→                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   136→                setTooltip({
   137→                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   138→                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   139→                  code,
   140→                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   141→                  participants,
   142→                  reports,
   143→                });
   144→              }}
   145→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   146→              className="cursor-pointer"
   147→            >
   148→              <path
   149→                d={d}
   150→                fill={fill}
   151→                stroke="#ffffff"
   152→                strokeWidth="2"
   153→                className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   154→              />
   155→              <text
   156→                x={labelX}
   157→                y={labelY - 6}
   158→                textAnchor="middle"
   159→                fontSize="11"
   160→                fontWeight="bold"
   161→                fill={textColor}
   162→                pointerEvents="none"
   163→              >
   164→                {code}
   165→              </text>
   166→              <text
   167→                x={labelX}
   168→                y={labelY + 8}
   169→                textAnchor="middle"
   170→                fontSize="10"
   171→                fill={textColor}
   172→                pointerEvents="none"
   173→              >
   174→                {participants}
   175→              </text>
   176→            </g>
   177→          );
   178→        })}
   179→      </svg>
   180→
   181→      {/* Tooltip */}
   182→      {tooltip && (
   183→        <div
   184→          className="absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
   185→          style={{
   186→            left: tooltip.x,
   187→            top: tooltip.y,
   188→            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',
   189→          }}
   190→        >
   191→          <div className="font-bold">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>
   192→          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>
   193→          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>
   194→        </div>
   195→      )}
   196→
   197→      {/* Legend */}
   198→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   199→        <span>0</span>
   200→        <div
   201→          className="h-3 w-32 rounded"
   202→          style={{
   203→            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',
   204→          }}
   205→        />
   206→        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>
   207→        <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400">Teilnehmer</span>
   208→      </div>
   209→    </div>
   210→  );
   211→}
   212→

   labelX: 550,\n    labelY: 97,\n  },\n};\n\nfunction getColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n}\n\nexport default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {\n  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{\n    x: number;\n    y: number;\n    code: string;\n    name: string;\n    participants: number;\n    reports: number;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));\n  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      <svg\n        viewBox=\"-5 -5 623 324\"\n        className=\"w-full h-auto\"\n        role=\"img\"\n        aria-label=\"Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland\"\n      >\n        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {\n          const bl = dataMap.get(code);\n          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;\n          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;\n          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);\n          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';\n\n          return (\n            <g\n              key={code}\n              onMouseEnter={(e) => {\n                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');\n                if (!svg) return;\n                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();\n                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();\n                pt.x = labelX;\n                pt.y = labelY;\n                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();\n                if (!ctm) return;\n                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);\n                setTooltip({\n                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,\n                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,\n                  code,\n                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,\n                  participants,\n                  reports,\n                });\n              }}\n              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}\n              className=\"cursor-pointer\"\n            >\n              <path\n                d={d}\n                fill={fill}\n                stroke=\"#ffffff\"\n                strokeWidth=\"2\"\n                className=\"transition-opacity hover:opacity-80\"\n              />\n              <text\n                x={labelX}\n                y={labelY - 5}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                fontSize=\"10\"\n                fontWeight=\"bold\"\n                fill={textColor}\n                pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              >\n                {code}\n              </text>\n              <text\n                x={labelX}\n                y={labelY + 7}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                fontSize=\"9\"\n                fill={textColor}\n                pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              >\n                {participants}\n              </text>\n            </g>\n          );\n        })}\n      </svg>\n\n      {/* Tooltip */}\n      {tooltip && (\n        <div\n          className=\"absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap\"\n          style={{\n            left: tooltip.x,\n            top: tooltip.y,\n            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',\n          }}\n        >\n          <div className=\"font-bold\">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>\n          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>\n          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Legend */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500\">\n        <span>0</span>\n        <div\n          className=\"h-3 w-32 rounded\"\n          style={{\n            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',\n          }}\n        />\n        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>\n        <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-400\">Teilnehmer</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

L604.45,108.6z\\\", // Burgenland: x range ~519-612, y range ~109-260\n  OE8: \\\"M251.29,228.77 ... z\\\", // Kärnten: x range ~254-444, y range ~226-314\n  OE3: \\\"M441.98,0.73 ... z\\\", // NÖ: x range ~395-612, y range ~0-190 (wraps Wien)\n  OE5: \\\"M347.18,30.34 ... z\\\", // OÖ: x range ~260-435, y range ~30-186\n  OE2: \\\"M268.29,123.02 ... z\\\", // Salzburg: x range ~206-358, y range ~118-247\n  OE6: \\\"M418.42,152.69 ... z\\\", // Steiermark: x range ~324-532, y range ~143-285\n  OE7: \\\"...\\\", // Tirol: x range ~51-268, y range ~120-260\n  OE9: \\\"M24,170.85 ... z\\\", // Vorarlberg: x range ~0-58, y range ~170-260\n  OE1: \\\"M550.84,84.85 ... z\\\", // Wien: x range ~534-565, y range ~84-109\n};\n\n// Manually computed visual centers based on bounding boxes from SVG data\nconst centers = {\n  OE1: { x: 550, y: 97 },    // Wien - tiny, inside NÖ\n  OE2: { x: 290, y: 183 },   // Salzburg\n  OE3: { x: 482, y: 55 },    // NÖ - upper part (avoiding Wien hole)\n  OE4: { x: 568, y: 185 },   // Burgenland - narrow, mid-height\n  OE5: { x: 375, y: 105 },   // OÖ\n  OE6: { x: 445, y: 218 },   // Steiermark\n  OE7: { x: 150, y: 200 },   // Tirol main body\n  OE8: { x: 350, y: 272 },   // Kärnten\n  OE9: { x: 30, y: 215 },    // Vorarlberg\n};\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(centers, null, 2));\n\"'",
  "description": "Compute visual centers for labels"
}

   104→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   105→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   106→
   107→  return (
   108→    <div className="relative">
   109→      <svg
   110→        viewBox="-5 -5 623 324"

   114→      >
   115→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   116→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   117→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   118→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   119→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   120→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   121→
   122→          return (
   123→            <g
   124→              key={code}
   125→              onMouseEnter={(e) => {

   129→                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   130→                pt.x = labelX;

   132→                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   133→                if (!ctm) return;
   134→                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   135→                setTooltip({
   136→                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   137→                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   138→                  code,
   139→                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   140→                  participants,
   141→                  reports,
   142→                });
   143→              }}

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     3→import { apiFetch } from '../services/api';
     4→import {
     5→  Chart as ChartJS,
     6→  CategoryScale, LinearScale, BarElement,

     8→  Title, Tooltip, Legend,
     9→} from 'chart.js';
    10→import { Bar, Pie } from 'react-chartjs-2';
    11→import { toPng } from 'html-to-image';

    13→ChartJS.register(CategoryScale, LinearScale, BarElement, ArcElement, PointElement, LineElement, Title, Tooltip, Legend);

    15→interface BezirkStat {
    16→  bundesland: string;
    17→  bundesland_code: string;
    18→  bezirk_code: string;
    19→  bezirk_name: string;
    20→  is_capital: number;

    22→  reports: number;
    23→}
    24→
    25→interface Participant {
    26→  callsign: string;
    27→  name: string | null;
    28→  bezirk_code: string | null;
    29→  bundesland_code: string | null;
    30→  bundesland_name: string | null;
    31→  report_count: number;
    32→}

    34→interface RepeaterStat {
    35→  short_name: string;

    37→}
    38→
    39→interface BlStat {

    43→  reports: number;
    44→}
    45→
    46→interface Stats {
    47→  totalParticipants: number;
    48→  totalReports: number;
    49→  perRepeater: RepeaterStat[];
    50→  bezirkStats: BezirkStat[];
    51→  blStats: BlStat[];
    52→  participants: Participant[];
    53→}
    54→
    55→export default function ReportsPage() {
    56→  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
    57→  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<any>(null);
    58→  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats | null>(null);
    59→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    60→  const [showParticipants, setShowParticipants] = useState(true);
    61→  const barChartRef = useRef<any>(null);
    62→  const pieChartRef = useRef<any>(null);
    63→  const barCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    64→  const pieCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    65→  const pageRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    66→  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false);
    67→
    68→  const createExportHeader = () => {
    69→    const header = document.createElement('div');
    70→    header.setAttribute('data-export-inject', 'true');
    71→    header.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:16px 24px;background:#1e3a5f;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:16px;';
    72→    const img = document.createElement('img');
    73→    img.src = '/bosarsa.jpeg';
    74→    img.style.cssText = 'height:36px;border-radius:4px;';
    75→    header.appendChild(img);
    76→    const text = document.createElement('div');
    77→    const title = document.createElement('div');
    78→    title.textContent = 'BOS-ARSA Log';
    79→    title.style.cssText = 'font-size:16px;font-weight:bold;color:#ffffff;letter-spacing:0.5px;';
    80→    text.appendChild(title);
    81→    const sub = document.createElement('div');
    82→    sub.textContent = 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit';
    83→    sub.style.cssText = 'font-size:10px;color:#93c5fd;letter-spacing:1px;';
    84→    text.appendChild(sub);
    85→    header.appendChild(text);
    86→    return header;
    87→  };
    88→
    89→  const createExportFooter = () => {
    90→    const footer = document.createElement('div');
    91→    footer.setAttribute('data-export-inject', 'true');
    92→    footer.style.cssText = 'text-align:center;padding:12px 0;margin-top:16px;border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;font-size:12px;color:#6b7280;';
    93→    footer.textContent = 'oeradio.at  \u00B7  bos-arsa.at';
    94→    return footer;
    95→  };
    96→
    97→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
    98→    if (!cardRef.current || !exercise) return;
    99→    setExporting(true);
   100→    const header = createExportHeader();
   101→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   102→    // Wrap the card temporarily
   103→    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
   104→    wrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:24px;background:#f3f4f6;';
   105→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;
   106→    const next = cardRef.current.nextSibling;
   107→    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cardRef.current);
   108→    wrapper.appendChild(header);
   109→    wrapper.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   110→    wrapper.appendChild(footer);
   111→    try {
   112→      const url = await toPng(wrapper, { backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6', pixelRatio: 2 });
   113→      const link = document.createElement('a');
   114→      link.download = filename;
   115→      link.href = url;
   116→      link.click();
   117→    } catch {} finally {
   118→      // Move card back to original position
   119→      if (next) parent.insertBefore(cardRef.current, next); else parent.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   120→      wrapper.remove();
   121→      setExporting(false);
   122→    }
   123→  };
   124→
   125→  const downloadFullPage = async () => {
   126→    if (!pageRef.current || !exercise) return;
   127→    setExporting(true);
   128→    const header = createExportHeader();
   129→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   130→    // Inject header/footer into actual DOM
   131→    pageRef.current.style.padding = '24px';
   132→    pageRef.current.insertBefore(header, pageRef.current.firstChild);
   133→    pageRef.current.appendChild(footer);
   134→    try {
   135→      const url = await toPng(pageRef.current, {
   136→        backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6',
   137→        pixelRatio: 2,
   138→        filter: (node) => {
   139→          if (node instanceof HTMLElement && node.dataset.noExport === 'true') return false;
   140→          return true;
   141→        },
   142→      });
   143→      const link = document.createElement('a');
   144→      link.download = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${exercise.date}.png`;
   145→      link.href = url;
   146→      link.click();
   147→    } catch {} finally {
   148→      header.remove();
   149→      footer.remove();
   150→      if (pageRef.current) pageRef.current.style.padding = '';
   151→      setExporting(false);
   152→    }
   153→  };
   154→
   155→  useEffect(() => {
   156→    if (!id) return;
   157→    Promise.all([
   158→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),
   159→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/stats`),
   160→    ]).then(([ex, st]) => {
   161→      setExercise(ex);
   162→      setStats(st);
   163→    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));
   164→  }, [id]);
   165→
   166→  if (loading) return <p className="text-gray-500">Laden...</p>;
   167→  if (!exercise) return <p className="text-red-500">Nicht gefunden</p>;
   168→
   169→  // Group bezirk stats by Bundesland
   170→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalParticipants: number; totalReports: number }> = {};
   171→  if (stats) {
   172→    for (const bz of stats.bezirkStats) {
   173→      if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   174→        byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0 };
   175→      }
   176→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   177→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalParticipants += bz.participants;
   178→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalReports += bz.reports;
   179→    }
   180→  }
   181→
   182→  const formatDate = (d: string) => {
   183→    const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   184→    return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   185→  };
   186→
   187→  return (
   188→    <div ref={pageRef} className="space-y-6">
   189→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   190→        <div className="min-w-0">
   191→          <h1 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Auswertung</h1>
   192→          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500 truncate">{exercise.name} — {formatDate(exercise.date)}</p>
   193→        </div>
   194→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0" data-no-export="true">
   195→          <button
   196→            onClick={downloadFullPage}
   197→            disabled={exporting}
   198→            className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
   199→          >
   200→            {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   201→          </button>
   202→          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}`} className="bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm">
   203→            Zurück
   204→          </Link>
   205→        </div>
   206→      </div>
   207→
   208→      {stats && (
   209→        <>
   210→          {/* Summary cards */}
   211→          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
   212→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   213→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalParticipants}</div>
   214→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Teilnehmer</div>
   215→            </div>
   216→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   217→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalReports}</div>
   218→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Rapporte</div>
   219→            </div>
   220→            {stats.perRepeater.map((r) => (
   221→              <div key={r.short_name} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   222→                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-700">{r.count}</div>
   223→                <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{r.short_name}</div>
   224→              </div>
   225→            ))}
   226→          </div>
   227→
   228→          {/* Charts */}
   229→          {(() => {
   230→            // Use bezirkStats if available, otherwise fall back to blStats (Bundesland level)
   231→            const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   232→              ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   233→              : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   234→            const chartLevel = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 ? 'Bezirk' : 'Bundesland';
   235→
   236→            return chartData.length > 0 ? (
   237→            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
   238→              {/* Bar chart: Stationen + Rapporte */}
   239→              <div ref={barCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   240→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   241→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   242→                    BOS-ARSA Übung am {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}
   243→                  </h2>
   244→                  <button
   245→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(barCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Stationen_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   246→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   247→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   248→                    data-no-export="true"
   249→                  >
   250→                    Download
   251→                  </button>
   252→                </div>
   253→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px]">
   254→                  <Bar
   255→                    ref={barChartRef}
   256→                    data={{
   257→                      labels: chartData.map(d => d.label),
   258→                      datasets: [
   259→                        {
   260→                          label: 'Stationen',
   261→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.participants),
   262→                          backgroundColor: '#1e3a5f',
   263→                        },
   264→                        {
   265→                          label: 'Rapporte',
   266→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.reports),
   267→                          backgroundColor: '#c8102e',
   268→                        },
   269→                      ],
   270→                    }}
   271→                    options={{
   272→                      responsive: true,
   273→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   274→                      plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } },
   275→                      scales: {
   276→                        x: { ticks: { maxRotation: 90, minRotation: 45, font: { size: 9 } } },
   277→                        y: { beginAtZero: true },
   278→                      },
   279→                    }}
   280→                  />
   281→                </div>
   282→              </div>
   283→
   284→              {/* Pie chart: Rapporte distribution */}
   285→              <div ref={pieCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   286→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   287→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   288→                    Rapporte-Verteilung nach {chartLevel}
   289→                  </h2>
   290→                  <button
   291→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(pieCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Verteilung_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   292→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   293→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   294→                    data-no-export="true"
   295→                  >
   296→                    Download
   297→                  </button>
   298→                </div>
   299→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px] flex items-center justify-center">
   300→                  <Pie
   301→                    ref={pieChartRef}
   302→                    data={{
   303→                      labels: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.label),
   304→                      datasets: [{
   305→                        data: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.reports),
   306→                        backgroundColor: [
   307→                          '#1e3a5f', '#c8102e', '#d97706', '#0d6efd', '#198754',
   308→                          '#6f42c1', '#fd7e14', '#20c997', '#0dcaf0', '#6c757d',
   309→                          '#2c5282', '#e8a317', '#c70039', '#4361ee', '#2d6a4f',
   310→                          '#9b59b6', '#e67e22', '#1abc9c', '#3498db', '#95a5a6',
   311→                        ],
   312→                      }],
   313→                    }}
   314→                    options={{
   315→                      responsive: true,
   316→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   317→                      plugins: {
   318→                        legend: { position: 'right', labels: { font: { size: 10 } } },
   319→                      },
   320→                    }}
   321→                  />
   322→                </div>
   323→              </div>
   324→            </div>
   325→          ) : null;
   326→          })()}
   327→
   328→          {/* Bundesland table (when no bezirk data) */}
   329→          {stats.bezirkStats.length === 0 && stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   330→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   331→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold">
   332→                Teilnehmer nach Bundesland
   333→              </div>
   334→              <table className="w-full text-sm">
   335→                <thead>
   336→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   337→                    <th className="px-4 py-2">Bundesland</th>
   338→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Teilnehmer</th>
   339→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Rapporte</th>
   340→                  </tr>
   341→                </thead>
   342→                <tbody>
   343→                  {stats.blStats.map(bl => (
   344→                    <tr key={bl.bundesland_code} className="border-t hover:bg-blue-50">
   345→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 font-medium">{bl.bundesland}</td>
   346→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.participants}</td>
   347→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.reports}</td>
   348→                    </tr>
   349→                  ))}
   350→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   351→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   352→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   353→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   354→                  </tr>
   355→                </tbody>
   356→              </table>
   357→            </div>
   358→          )}
   359→
   360→          {/* Bezirk/Bundesland table */}
   361→          {stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 && (
   362→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   363→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold">
   364→                Nebenstationen nach Bezirk
   365→              </div>
   366→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   367→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   368→                <thead>
   369→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   370→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bundesland / Bezirk</th>
   371→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Teiln.</th>
   372→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Rapp.</th>
   373→                  </tr>
   374→                </thead>
   375→                <tbody>
   376→                  {Object.entries(byBl).map(([blCode, bl]) => (
   377→                    <>
   378→                      {/* Bundesland header */}
   379→                      <tr key={`bl-${blCode}`} className="bg-gray-50 font-medium border-t">
   380→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5">{bl.name}</td>
   381→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalParticipants}</td>
   382→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalReports}</td>
   383→                      </tr>
   384→                      {/* Bezirk rows */}
   385→                      {bl.bezirke.map(bz => (
   386→                        <tr key={bz.bezirk_code} className="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-50">
   387→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 pl-8">
   388→                            <span className={`inline-block text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white mr-2 ${bz.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   389→                              {bz.bezirk_code}
   390→                            </span>
   391→                            <span className="text-gray-600">{bz.bezirk_name}</span>
   392→                          </td>
   393→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.participants}</td>
   394→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.reports}</td>
   395→                        </tr>
   396→                      ))}
   397→                    </>
   398→                  ))}
   399→                  {/* Gesamt */}
   400→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   401→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   402→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   403→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   404→                  </tr>
   405→                </tbody>
   406→              </table>
   407→              </div>
   408→            </div>
   409→          )}
   410→
   411→          {/* Participants list */}
   412→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   413→            <div
   414→              onClick={() => setShowParticipants(v => !v)}
   415→              className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] flex items-center justify-between"
   416→            >
   417→              <span>Teilnehmer-Liste ({stats.participants.length})</span>
   418→              <span className="text-xs">{showParticipants ? '▼' : '▶'}</span>
   419→            </div>
   420→            {showParticipants && (
   421→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   422→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   423→                <thead>
   424→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   425→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 w-8">#</th>
   426→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Rufzeichen</th>
   427→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Name</th>
   428→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>
   429→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden md:table-cell">Bundesland</th>
   430→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   431→                  </tr>
   432→                </thead>
   433→                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   434→                  {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   435→                    <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   436→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   437→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}</td>
   438→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   439→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   440→                        {p.bezirk_code && (
   441→                          <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   442→                        )}
   443→                      </td>
   444→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   445→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   446→                    </tr>
   447→                  ))}
   448→                </tbody>
   449→              </table>
   450→              </div>
   451→            )}
   452→          </div>
   453→
   454→          {/* Export buttons */}
   455→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   456→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   457→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   458→              <button
   459→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   460→                disabled={exporting}
   461→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   462→              >
   463→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'PNG — Download Auswertung'}
   464→              </button>
   465→              {exercise.oe_link_enabled !== 0 && (
   466→                <a
   467→                  href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}
   468→                  className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   469→                  target="_blank"
   470→                >
   471→                  TXT — OE-Link
   472→                </a>
   473→              )}
   474→              <a
   475→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}
   476→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   477→                target="_blank"
   478→              >
   479→                TXT — Frequenzen
   480→              </a>
   481→              <a
   482→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/combined`}
   483→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   484→                target="_blank"
   485→              >
   486→                TXT — Kombiniert
   487→              </a>
   488→            </div>
   489→          </div>
   490→        </>
   491→      )}
   492→    </div>
   493→  );
   494→}
   495→

    83→
    84→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    85→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    86→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    87→  const t = value / max;
    88→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    89→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    90→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
    91→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    92→}
    93→
    94→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
    95→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    96→    x: number;
    97→    y: number;
    98→    code: string;
    99→    name: string;
   100→    participants: number;
   101→    reports: number;
   102→  } | null>(null);
   103→
   104→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   105→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   106→

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile a comprehensive report:

## Exploration Complete - Neighboring Countries Analysis

I've thoroughly explored the codebase to understand how neighboring countries are currently handled. Here's my detailed findings:

---

### 1. **Bundesländer Seeding (`server/src/db/seed.ts`, lines 12-38)**

The database **already has entries for neighboring countries**:

```typescript
const bundeslaender = [
  ['01', 'OE1 Wien', 1, 0],
  ['02', 'OE2 Salzburg', 2, 0],
  // ... OE3-OE9 (Austrian states)
  ['10', 'Slowenien', 10, 1],          // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['11', 'Tschechien', 11, 1],         // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['12', 'Deutschland', 12, 1],        // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['13', 'Italien', 13, 1],            // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['14', 'Slowakei', 14, 1],           // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['15', 'Kroatien', 15, 1],           // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['16', 'Liechtenstein', 16, 1],      // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['17', 'Schweiz', 17, 1],            // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
  ['18', 'Ungarn', 18, 1],             // NEIGHBORING COUNTRY
];
```

**Key observations:**
- Codes 10-18 are reserved for international neighbors
- They all have `is_international = 1` flag (the 4th parameter)
- Austrian states (01-09) have `is_international = 0`

---

### 2. **Repeaters with International Callsigns (`server/src/db/seed.ts`, lines 261-263)**

There are already 2 repeaters mapped to Slowenien (code '10'):

```typescript
// ─── International ───
['Struška 70cm', 'Struška', '70cm', 'S55UJE', 439.325, -7.6, 123.0, null, 'repeater', 0, ++sortIdx, '10'],
['Hochstuhl 70cm', 'Hochstuhl', '70cm', null, 439.3625, -7.6, 123.0, null, 'repeater', 0, ++sortIdx, '10'],
```

**Callsign S55UJE** is a Slovenian amateur radio callsign (S5 prefix).

---

### 3. **Callsign-to-Bundesland Mapping Logic (`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, lines 128-141)**

The exact SQL query in the `blStats` computation:

```sql
const blStats = db.prepare(`
  SELECT
    COALESCE(bl.name, 'OE' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1), 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
    COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)) as bundesland_code,
    COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
  FROM signal_reports sr
  JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
  LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    OR (o.bundesland_code IS NULL AND bl.code = '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
  WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
  GROUP BY COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
  ORDER BY bundesland_code
`).all(eid);
```

**How it works:**
- Extracts **character 3 of the callsign**: `SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)`
  - For OE8YML → extracts **8**
  - For S55UJE → extracts **5**
- Prepends **'0'** to create bundesland_code: `'0' || SUBSTR(...)` creates codes like "08", "05"
- Falls back to explicit `o.bundesland_code` if set
- If no explicit code and can't parse from callsign → classified as 'Sonstige' (other)

---

### 4. **ReportsPage AustriaMap Data Mapping (`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`, lines 246-251)**

```typescript
<AustriaMap
  data={stats.blStats.map((bl) => ({
    code: `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}`,
    participants: bl.participants,
    reports: bl.reports,
  }))}
/>
```

**The mapping:**
- Takes `bundesland_code` from backend (e.g., "08", "10")
- Converts to integer: `parseInt("08", 10)` → 8
- Prepends "OE": `"OE" + 8` → **"OE8"**
- Only codes 01-09 are rendered (Kärnten through Wien)
- **International codes 10-18 are silently dropped** because `AustriaMap.tsx` only has `BL_PATHS` for OE1-OE9

---

### 5. **Database Schema (`server/src/db/schema.ts`)**

**bundeslaender table:**
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bundeslaender (
  code TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  sort_order INTEGER,
  is_international INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);
```

**repeaters table:**
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS repeaters (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  short_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  site_name TEXT,
  band TEXT,
  callsign TEXT,
  frequency_mhz REAL,
  offset_mhz REAL,
  ctcss_hz REAL,
  burst_hz REAL,
  type TEXT DEFAULT 'repeater',
  is_linked INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  sort_order INTEGER,
  bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code),
  is_custom INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);
```

**operators table:**
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operators (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  callsign TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  -- ... other fields ...
  bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code),
  -- ... other fields ...
);
```

---

### 6. **Summary of Current State**

| Feature | Status | Details |
|---------|--------|---------|
| **Bundesländer table** | ✓ Ready | Codes 10-18 pre-seeded for 9 neighbors + is_international flag |
| **Repeater linking** | ✓ Ready | 2 Slovenian repeaters in code 10 |
| **Callsign→country logic** | ✓ Functional | Extracts 3rd char of callsign, prepends '0' to create code |
| **Backend stats computation** | ✓ Functional | blStats query groups by bundesland_code, handles nulls via callsign parsing |
| **Frontend rendering** | ⚠ Limited | AustriaMap component only displays OE1-OE9, international codes (10-18) are filtered out |
| **Callsign-to-Bundesland** | ✓ Flexible | Uses explicit bundesland_code if set, otherwise parses from callsign position 3 |

---

### Key Files and Paths

1. **Bundesländer Seed Data**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/seed.ts` (lines 13-33)
2. **BlStats SQL Query**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts` (lines 128-141)
3. **Data Transformation for Map**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (lines 246-251)
4. **Schema Definition**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts` (lines 5-10, 42-57, 19-40)
5. **Map Component**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` (lines 13-23 for BL_NAMES, lines 27-82 for BL_PATHS)
