    82→    text.appendChild(title);
    83→    const sub = document.createElement('div');
    84→    sub.textContent = 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit';
    85→    sub.style.cssText = 'font-size:10px;color:#93c5fd;letter-spacing:1px;';
    86→    text.appendChild(sub);
    87→    header.appendChild(text);
    88→    return header;
    89→  };
    90→
    91→  const createExportFooter = () => {
    92→    const footer = document.createElement('div');
    93→    footer.setAttribute('data-export-inject', 'true');
    94→    footer.style.cssText = 'text-align:center;padding:12px 0;margin-top:16px;border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;font-size:12px;color:#6b7280;';
    95→    footer.textContent = 'oeradio.at  \u00B7  bos-arsa.at';
    96→    return footer;
    97→  };
    98→
    99→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
   100→    if (!cardRef.current || !exercise) return;
   101→    setExporting(true);
   102→    const header = createExportHeader();
   103→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   104→    // Wrap the card temporarily
   105→    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
   106→    wrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:24px;background:#f3f4f6;';
   107→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;
   108→    const next = cardRef.current.nextSibling;
   109→    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cardRef.current);
   110→    wrapper.appendChild(header);
   111→    wrapper.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   112→    wrapper.appendChild(footer);
   113→    try {
   114→      const url = await toPng(wrapper, { backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6', pixelRatio: 2 });
   115→      const link = document.createElement('a');
   116→      link.download = filename;
   117→      link.href = url;
   118→      link.click();
   119→    } catch {} finally {
   120→      // Move card back to original position
   121→      if (next) parent.insertBefore(cardRef.current, next); else parent.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   122→      wrapper.remove();
   123→      setExporting(false);
   124→    }
   125→  };
   126→
   127→  const downloadFullPage = async () => {
   128→    if (!pageRef.current || !exercise) return;
   129→    setExporting(true);
   130→    const header = createExportHeader();
   131→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   132→    // Inject header/footer into actual DOM
   133→    pageRef.current.style.padding = '24px';
   134→    pageRef.current.insertBefore(header, pageRef.current.firstChild);
   135→    pageRef.current.appendChild(footer);
   136→    try {
   137→      const url = await toPng(pageRef.current, {
   138→        backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6',
   139→        pixelRatio: 2,
   140→        filter: (node) => {
   141→          if (node instanceof HTMLElement && node.dataset.noExport === 'true') return false;
   142→          return true;
   143→        },
   144→      });
   145→      const link = document.createElement('a');
   146→      link.download = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${exercise.date}.png`;
   147→      link.href = url;
   148→      link.click();
   149→    } catch {} finally {
   150→      header.remove();
   151→      footer.remove();
   152→      if (pageRef.current) pageRef.current.style.padding = '';
   153→      setExporting(false);
   154→    }
   155→  };
   156→
   157→  useEffect(() => {
   158→    if (!id) return;
   159→    Promise.all([
   160→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),
   161→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/stats`),
   162→    ]).then(([ex, st]) => {
   163→      setExercise(ex);
   164→      setStats(st);
   165→    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));
   166→  }, [id]);
   167→
   168→  if (loading) return <p className="text-gray-500">Laden...</p>;
   169→  if (!exercise) return <p className="text-red-500">Nicht gefunden</p>;
   170→
   171→  // Group bezirk stats by Bundesland
   172→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalParticipants: number; totalReports: number }> = {};
   173→  if (stats) {
   174→    for (const bz of stats.bezirkStats) {
   175→      if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   176→        byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0 };
   177→      }
   178→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   179→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalParticipants += bz.participants;
   180→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalReports += bz.reports;
   181→    }
   182→  }
   183→
   184→  const formatDate = (d: string) => {
   185→    const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   186→    return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   187→  };
   188→
   189→  return (
   190→    <div ref={pageRef} className="space-y-6">
   191→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   192→        <div className="min-w-0">
   193→          <h1 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Auswertung</h1>
   194→          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500 truncate">{exercise.name} — {formatDate(exercise.date)}</p>
   195→        </div>
   196→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0" data-no-export="true">
   197→          <button
   198→            onClick={downloadFullPage}
   199→            disabled={exporting}
   200→            className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
   201→          >
   202→            {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   203→          </button>
   204→          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}`} className="bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm">
   205→            Zurück
   206→          </Link>
   207→        </div>
   208→      </div>
   209→
   210→      {stats && (
   211→        <>
   212→          {/* Summary cards */}
   213→          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
   214→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   215→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalParticipants}</div>
   216→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Teilnehmer</div>
   217→            </div>
   218→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   219→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalReports}</div>
   220→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Rapporte</div>
   221→            </div>
   222→            {stats.perRepeater.map((r) => (
   223→              <div key={r.short_name} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   224→                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-700">{r.count}</div>
   225→                <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{r.short_name}</div>
   226→              </div>
   227→            ))}
   228→          </div>
   229→
   230→          {/* Austria Map */}
   231→          {stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   232→            <div ref={mapCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   233→              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   234→                <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   235→                  Teilnehmer nach Bundesland
   236→                </h2>
   237→                <button
   238→                  onClick={() => downloadChart(mapCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Karte_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   239→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   240→                  title="Karte als PNG herunterladen"
   241→                  data-no-export="true"
   242→                >
   243→                  Download
   244→                </button>
   245→              </div>
   246→              <AustriaMap
   247→                data={stats.blStats.map((bl) => ({
   248→                  code: `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}`,
   249→                  participants: bl.participants,
   250→                  reports: bl.reports,
   251→                }))}
   252→              />
   253→            </div>
   254→          )}
   255→
   256→          {/* Charts */}
   257→          {(() => {
   258→            // Use bezirkStats if available, otherwise fall back to blStats (Bundesland level)
   259→            const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   260→              ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   261→              : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   262→            const chartLevel = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 ? 'Bezirk' : 'Bundesland';
   263→
   264→            return chartData.length > 0 ? (
   265→            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
   266→              {/* Bar chart: Stationen + Rapporte */}
   267→              <div ref={barCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   268→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   269→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   270→                    BOS-ARSA Übung am {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}
   271→                  </h2>
   272→                  <button
   273→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(barCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Stationen_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   274→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   275→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   276→                    data-no-export="true"
   277→                  >
   278→                    Download
   279→                  </button>
   280→                </div>
   281→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px]">
   282→                  <Bar

   290→                          backgroundColor: '#1e3a5f',
   291→                        },
   292→                        {
   293→                          label: 'Rapporte',
   294→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.reports),
   295→                          backgroundColor: '#c8102e',
   296→                        },
   297→                      ],
   298→                    }}
   299→                    options={{
   300→                      responsive: true,

   305→                        y: { beginAtZero: true },
   306→                      },
   307→                    }}
   308→                  />
   309→                </div>
   310→              </div>
   311→
   312→              {/* Pie chart: Rapporte distribution */}
   313→              <div ref={pieCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   314→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   315→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   316→                    Rapporte-Verteilung nach {chartLevel}
   317→                  </h2>
   318→                  <button
   319→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(pieCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Verteilung_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   320→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   321→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   322→                    data-no-export="true"
   323→                  >
   324→                    Download
   325→                  </button>
   326→                </div>
   327→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px] flex items-center justify-center">
   328→                  <Pie
   329→                    ref={pieChartRef}
   330→                    data={{
   331→                      labels: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.label),
   332→                      datasets: [{
   333→                        data: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.reports),
   334→                        backgroundColor: [
   335→                          '#1e3a5f', '#c8102e', '#d97706', '#0d6efd', '#198754',
   336→                          '#6f42c1', '#fd7e14', '#20c997', '#0dcaf0', '#6c757d',
   337→                          '#2c5282', '#e8a317', '#c70039', '#4361ee', '#2d6a4f',
   338→                          '#9b59b6', '#e67e22', '#1abc9c', '#3498db', '#95a5a6',
   339→                        ],
   340→                      }],
   341→                    }}
   342→                    options={{
   343→                      responsive: true,
   344→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   345→                      plugins: {
   346→                        legend: { position: 'right', labels: { font: { size: 10 } } },
   347→                      },
   348→                    }}
   349→                  />
   350→                </div>
   351→              </div>
   352→            </div>
   353→          ) : null;
   354→          })()}
   355→
   356→          {/* Bundesland table (when no bezirk data) */}

   361→              </div>
   362→              <table className="w-full text-sm">
   363→                <thead>
   364→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">

   366→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Teilnehmer</th>

   368→                  </tr>
   369→                </thead>
   370→                <tbody>

   375→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.reports}</td>
   376→                    </tr>
   377→                  ))}
   378→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   379→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   380→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   381→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   382→                  </tr>

   384→              </table>

   388→          {/* Bezirk/Bundesland table */}

   401→                  </tr>
   402→                </thead>

   419→                            <span className="text-gray-600">{bz.bezirk_name}</span>
   420→                          </td>
   421→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.participants}</td>
   422→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.reports}</td>
   423→                        </tr>
   424→                      ))}
   425→                    </>
   426→                  ))}
   427→                  {/* Gesamt */}
   428→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   429→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>

   431→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>

   438→
   439→          {/* Participants list */}
   440→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">

   442→              onClick={() => setShowParticipants(v => !v)}

   444→            >
   445→              <span>Teilnehmer-Liste ({stats.participants.length})</span>
   446→              <span className="text-xs">{showParticipants ? '▼' : '▶'}</span>
   447→            </div>
   448→            {showParticipants && (
   449→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   450→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">

   456→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>

   458→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   459→                  </tr>
   460→                </thead>
   461→                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   462→                  {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   463→                    <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">

   466→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   467→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   468→                        {p.bezirk_code && (
   469→                          <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   470→                        )}
   471→                      </td>
   472→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   473→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   474→                    </tr>
   475→                  ))}
   476→                </tbody>
   477→              </table>
   478→              </div>
   479→            )}
   480→          </div>
   481→
   482→          {/* Export buttons */}
   483→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   484→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   485→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   486→              <button
   487→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   488→                disabled={exporting}
   489→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   490→              >
   491→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'PNG — Download Auswertung'}
   492→              </button>
   493→              {exercise.oe_link_enabled !== 0 && (
   494→                <a
   495→                  href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}
   496→                  className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   497→                  target="_blank"
   498→                >
   499→                  TXT — OE-Link
   500→                </a>
   501→              )}
   502→              <a
   503→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}
   504→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   505→                target="_blank"
   506→              >
   507→                TXT — Frequenzen
   508→              </a>
   509→              <a
   510→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/combined`}
   511→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   512→                target="_blank"
   513→              >
   514→                TXT — Kombiniert
   515→              </a>
   516→            </div>
   517→          </div>
   518→        </>
   519→      )}
   520→    </div>
   521→  );
   522→}

    83→
    84→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    85→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    86→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    87→  const t = value / max;
    88→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    89→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    90→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
    91→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    92→}
    93→
    94→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
    95→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    96→    x: number;
    97→    y: number;
    98→    code: string;
    99→    name: string;
   100→    participants: number;
   101→    reports: number;
   102→  } | null>(null);
   103→
   104→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   105→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   106→
   107→  return (
   108→    <div className="relative">
   109→      <svg
   110→        viewBox="-5 -5 623 324"
   111→        className="w-full h-auto"
   112→        role="img"
   113→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland"
   114→      >
   115→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   116→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   117→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   118→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   119→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   120→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   121→
   122→          return (
   123→            <g
   124→              key={code}
   125→              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
   126→                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   127→                if (!svg) return;
   128→                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   129→                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   130→                pt.x = labelX;
   131→                pt.y = labelY;
   132→                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   133→                if (!ctm) return;
   134→                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   135→                setTooltip({
   136→                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   137→                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   138→                  code,
   139→                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   140→                  participants,
   141→                  reports,
   142→                });
   143→              }}
   144→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   145→              className="cursor-pointer"
   146→            >
   147→              <path
   148→                d={d}
   149→                fill={fill}
   150→                stroke="#ffffff"
   151→                strokeWidth="2"
   152→                className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   153→              />
   154→              <text
   155→                x={labelX}
   156→                y={labelY - 5}
   157→                textAnchor="middle"
   158→                fontSize="10"
   159→                fontWeight="bold"
   160→                fill={textColor}
   161→                pointerEvents="none"
   162→              >
   163→                {code}
   164→              </text>
   165→              <text
   166→                x={labelX}
   167→                y={labelY + 7}

   178→      </svg>
   179→
   180→      {/* Tooltip */}
   181→      {tooltip && (
   182→        <div
   183→          className="absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
   184→          style={{
   185→            left: tooltip.x,
   186→            top: tooltip.y,
   187→            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',
   188→          }}
   189→        >
   190→          <div className="font-bold">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>
   191→          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>
   192→          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>
   193→        </div>
   194→      )}
   195→
   196→      {/* Legend */}
   197→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   198→        <span>0</span>
   199→        <div
   200→          className="h-3 w-32 rounded"
   201→          style={{
   202→            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',
   203→          }}
   204→        />
   205→        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>
   206→        <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400">Teilnehmer</span>
   207→      </div>
   208→    </div>
   209→  );
   210→}

     8→// List all exercises with summary stats
     9→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {

    14→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count

    30→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    31→  res.status(201).json(exercise);
    32→});
    33→
    34→// Get full exercise with reports
    35→exercisesRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    36→  const db = getDb();
    37→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    38→  if (!exercise) {
    39→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
    40→    return;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
    44→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
    45→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked,
    46→      r.bundesland_code, bl.name as bundesland_name
    47→    FROM exercise_repeaters er
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = er.repeater_id
    49→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = r.bundesland_code
    50→    WHERE er.exercise_id = ?
    51→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, r.sort_order
    52→  `).all(req.params.id);
    53→
    54→  const reports = db.prepare(`

    57→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    58→    FROM signal_reports sr
    59→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    60→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    61→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    62→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    63→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, o.callsign
    64→  `).all(req.params.id);
    65→
    66→  const attendance = db.prepare(`

    70→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    71→    ORDER BY o.callsign

   103→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
   104→    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count

   106→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   107→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL

   115→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   116→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   117→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   118→    FROM signal_reports sr
   119→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id

   131→      COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)) as bundesland_code,
   132→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   133→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   134→    FROM signal_reports sr
   135→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   136→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   137→      OR (o.bundesland_code IS NULL AND bl.code = '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))

   139→    GROUP BY COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
   140→    ORDER BY bundesland_code
   141→  `).all(eid);
   142→
   143→  // Participant list: all operators with their report counts

   147→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
   148→    FROM signal_reports sr
   149→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   150→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code

## Detailed Implementation Plan: Neighboring Countries on Austria Map with Callsign-to-Country Mapping

### Overview of the Problem

The system currently has three interconnected bugs/gaps:

1. **Wrong callsign-to-Bundesland mapping for international callsigns**: The SQL fallback `'0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)` only works for Austrian OE callsigns. A Slovenian callsign like `S55UJE` produces `'0' || '5'` = `'05'` (Oberösterreich), which is wrong. It should map to code `'10'` (Slowenien).

2. **ReportsPage drops international data**: Line 248 of `ReportsPage.tsx` converts bundesland_code to map key via `` `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}` ``. Code `"10"` becomes `"OE10"`, which has no path in the Austria SVG. The data is silently lost.

3. **No country outlines on the map**: `AustriaMap.tsx` only contains SVG paths for the 9 Austrian Bundeslaender. Neighboring countries have no visual representation.

---

### Step-by-Step Implementation Plan

#### PHASE 1: Server-Side Callsign-to-Country Utility

**Step 1.1: Create `server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts`**

Create a new utility module with a function `callsignToCountryCode(callsign: string): string | null` that maps a ham radio callsign prefix to the correct `bundeslaender.code` value.

The mapping rules (based on the requirements):

```typescript
// Prefix-to-code mapping
// Austrian: OE1-OE9 -> "01"-"09"
// International:
//   S5*        -> "10" (Slowenien)
//   OK*, OL*   -> "11" (Tschechien)
//   D[A-R]*    -> "12" (Deutschland) - DL, DK, DJ, DO, DB, DC, DD, DF, DG, DH, etc.
//   I*, IK*, IZ*, IW* -> "13" (Italien)
//   OM*        -> "14" (Slowakei)
//   9A*        -> "15" (Kroatien)
//   HB0*       -> "16" (Liechtenstein) - must check before HB
//   HB[^0]*    -> "17" (Schweiz)
//   HA*, HG*   -> "18" (Ungarn)
```

The function should:
- Accept an uppercase callsign string
- Match the longest prefix first (HB0 before HB)
- Return the bundeslaender code string or null if no match
- Handle the OE case: extract digit at position 2 (0-indexed), prepend "0" -> gives "01"-"09"

Implementation logic:
```typescript
export function callsignToCountryCode(callsign: string): string | null {
  const cs = callsign.toUpperCase();
  // Austrian
  if (cs.startsWith('OE') && cs.length >= 3) {
    const digit = cs[2];
    if (digit >= '1' && digit <= '9') return `0${digit}`;
  }
  // Liechtenstein must come before Switzerland
  if (cs.startsWith('HB0')) return '16';
  // Switzerland
  if (cs.startsWith('HB')) return '17';
  // Slovenia
  if (cs.startsWith('S5')) return '10';
  // Czech Republic
  if (cs.startsWith('OK') || cs.startsWith('OL')) return '11';
  // Germany (all D-block: DA-DR)
  if (cs.startsWith('D') && cs.length >= 2 && cs[1] >= 'A' && cs[1] <= 'R') return '12';
  // Italy
  if (cs.startsWith('I')) return '13';
  // Slovakia
  if (cs.startsWith('OM')) return '14';
  // Croatia
  if (cs.startsWith('9A')) return '15';
  // Hungary
  if (cs.startsWith('HA') || cs.startsWith('HG')) return '18';
  return null;
}
```

**Note on German prefixes**: The German amateur radio prefix block is DA-DR (D followed by A through R). This covers DL, DK, DJ, DO, DB, DC, DD, DF, DG, DH, and others. Using `cs[1] >= 'A' && cs[1] <= 'R'` is correct and comprehensive.

**Step 1.2: Apply callsign mapping in operator creation**

Modify `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/operators.ts`, in the `POST /` handler (line 56-77). Currently, if `bundesland_code` is not provided by the client, it is stored as `null`. After this change:

- Import `callsignToCountryCode`
- When `bundesland_code` is not provided (or null), auto-derive it from the callsign using the utility
- This ensures all new operators get a correct bundesland_code

The change in the POST handler (around line 67):
```typescript
const derivedBl = bundesland_code || callsignToCountryCode(callsign.toUpperCase());
// ... use derivedBl instead of bundesland_code
```

Also apply the same logic in `getOrCreateOperator` -- but that is a client-side function that calls the server POST. The server POST is the right place since it centralizes the logic.

**Step 1.3: Fix the stats SQL queries in exercises.ts**

In `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, three queries use the broken `'0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)` fallback:

1. **blStats query (lines 128-141)**: Replace the COALESCE fallback with a proper SQL CASE expression or, better yet, simply rely on `o.bundesland_code` now that it is always populated. However, for backward compatibility with existing operators who might have null bundesland_code, we need a migration.

2. **participants query (lines 144-155)**: Same issue with the `bl2` join.

3. **operators search (operators.ts line 17)**: Same issue.

The cleanest approach: Rather than making the SQL more complex, add a **one-time migration** that backfills `bundesland_code` for all existing operators, then simplify the SQL to just use `o.bundesland_code`.

**Step 1.4: Add migration to backfill operator bundesland_code**

In `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts`, add a new migration block after existing migrations. This migration should:

1. Select all operators where `bundesland_code IS NULL`
2. For each, run `callsignToCountryCode(callsign)` and update

Since `schema.ts` runs SQL (not TypeScript logic easily), the approach is:
- Add a SQL UPDATE with CASE WHEN for the known OE patterns, plus a TypeScript loop for international callsigns

However, to keep it simple and use the utility function, the migration is better placed as a function called from `schema.ts`:

```typescript
// In schema.ts, after existing migrations:
import { callsignToCountryCode } from '../utils/callsignCountry.js';

// Backfill bundesland_code for operators
const nullBlOps = db.prepare("SELECT id, callsign FROM operators WHERE bundesland_code IS NULL").all() as any[];
if (nullBlOps.length > 0) {
  const updateBl = db.prepare("UPDATE operators SET bundesland_code = ? WHERE id = ?");
  const backfill = db.transaction(() => {
    for (const op of nullBlOps) {
      const code = callsignToCountryCode(op.callsign);
      if (code) updateBl.run(code, op.id);
    }
  });
  backfill();
}
```

**Step 1.5: Simplify the SQL queries**

After the migration ensures all operators have `bundesland_code`, simplify the SQL:

In **exercises.ts** `blStats` query (lines 128-141), replace with:
```sql
SELECT
  COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
  COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '99') as bundesland_code,
  COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
  COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
FROM signal_reports sr
JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
ORDER BY bundesland_code
```

In **exercises.ts** `participants` query (lines 144-155), simplify the join:
```sql
SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
  bl.name as bundesland_name,
  COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
FROM signal_reports sr
JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
GROUP BY sr.operator_id
ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
```

In **operators.ts** search query (line 17), simplify:
```sql
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
```

Remove all the `OR (o.bundesland_code IS NULL AND bl.code = '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))` constructs.

**Step 3.2: Add SVG paths for neighboring countries**

This is the most technically involved step. The existing Austrian paths are geo-calibrated to a specific Mercator projection with known geographic bounds:

- geoViewBox: `"9.500931 49.015279 17.163182 46.371323"` (lon_min, lat_max, lon_max, lat_min)
- SVG viewBox: `"0 0 612.94 313.55"` (approximately, with -5 -5 padding)

The projection formulas are:
```
svgX = (lon - 9.500931) / (17.163182 - 9.500931) * 612.94
svgY = (49.015279 - lat) / (49.015279 - 46.371323) * 313.55
```

Wait -- this is a simple linear (equirectangular) projection, NOT Mercator. For the relatively small geographic extent of Austria and its neighbors, the difference is minimal, so this should work fine.

**Recommended approach for generating country paths:**

1. Obtain GeoJSON for each neighboring country (from Natural Earth dataset, freely available)
2. Write a one-time Node.js script (not committed, or placed in `scripts/`) that:
   - Reads GeoJSON for each country
   - Applies the projection formula above to convert lat/lon to SVG x/y
   - Clips paths to a slightly expanded viewBox (to show partial outlines of neighbors)
   - Outputs SVG path `d` strings
3. Hardcode the resulting paths into `AustriaMap.tsx`

The viewBox needs to expand to show neighboring areas. Currently it is `-5 -5 623 324`. To show partial outlines of Germany (north), Czech Republic (northeast), Slovakia (east), Hungary (east/southeast), Slovenia (south), Croatia (south), Italy (southwest), Switzerland (west), and Liechtenstein (west):

The geographic extent needs to grow. Current bounds: longitude 9.50-17.16, latitude 46.37-49.02. Neighbors extend to roughly:
- West: ~5.5 (Switzerland/Liechtenstein)
- East: ~22.5 (Hungary/Slovakia)
- North: ~51.5 (Czech Republic/Germany)
- South: ~42.5 (Croatia/Italy)

However, showing the FULL extent of all countries would make Austria too small. Instead, expand by about 2-3 degrees in each direction:
- lon: 7.0 to 19.5
- lat: 44.5 to 50.5

This means the viewBox and projection would need updating. But this changes the coordinate system for the existing Austrian paths, so the existing paths would need to be repositioned.

**Alternative (simpler) approach**: Keep the existing viewBox and projection, but render neighboring country shapes that are clipped to the edges. Parts of Germany and Czech Republic appear at the top, Slovenia at the bottom, etc. This gives geographic context without changing the existing coordinate system.

Actually, the simplest approach that avoids repositioning existing paths is:
1. Expand the SVG viewBox slightly (maybe 50-100px in each direction)
2. Use the same projection formula (but now coordinates that are outside the original bounds will have negative x or y > 613, or y < 0, etc.)
3. Render the neighbor country paths BEHIND the Austrian paths
4. Apply an SVG `clipPath` to restrict the rendering area, or simply let them overflow and be visually clipped by the viewBox

Specifically:
- Keep the projection formula as is (based on the original geoViewBox)
- Points outside Austria will naturally fall outside the 0-613, 0-314 range
- Expand the viewBox to something like `-100 -80 823 504` to show neighboring areas
- Neighboring countries will appear partially, giving geographic context

Let me compute where neighboring capital cities would land:
- Munich (11.58, 48.14): svgX = (11.58-9.50)/(17.16-9.50)*612.94 = 166.5, svgY = (49.02-48.14)/(49.02-46.37)*313.55 = 104.3 -- within bounds, good
- Prague (14.42, 50.08): svgX = 393.3, svgY = -100.2 -- above the map, would need expanded viewBox
- Bratislava (17.11, 48.15): svgX = 608.0, svgY = 103.1 -- right at the right edge
- Budapest (19.04, 47.50): svgX = 762.3, svgY = 180.3 -- far right
- Ljubljana (14.51, 46.05): svgX = 399.5, svgY = 352.4 -- below map
- Zagreb (15.98, 45.81): svgX = 516.8, svgY = 380.8 -- below map

So the viewBox expansion should be roughly: `-100 -120 913 550` to show meaningful portions of all neighbors.

**Step 3.3: Rendering approach in AustriaMap.tsx**

The component should render in two layers:

1. **Background layer** (neighboring countries): Light gray fill (`#e5e7eb` or similar), thin gray stroke. These paths are rendered first. If a country has participant data, fill with the same color scale but at 50% opacity or a lighter shade.

2. **Foreground layer** (Austrian Bundeslaender): Same as current -- white stroke, blue fill scale.

3. **Labels**: Austrian states show `OE1`-`OE9` as currently. Neighboring countries only show their short code (DE, CZ, etc.) if they have participant data.

Structurally:
```tsx
{/* Neighbor countries (background) */}
{Object.entries(NEIGHBOR_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
  const bl = dataMap.get(code);
  const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
  const fill = participants > 0 ? getColor(participants, maxParticipants) : '#e5e7eb';
  return (
    <g key={code}>
      <path d={d} fill={fill} stroke="#d1d5db" strokeWidth="1" opacity={0.6} />
      {participants > 0 && (
        <>
          <text ...>{BL_SHORT[code]}</text>
          <text ...>{participants}</text>
        </>
      )}
    </g>
  );
})}

{/* Austrian Bundeslaender (foreground) */}
{Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(...)} // existing code, keys changed to "01"-"09"
```

Separate the path data into two maps:
- `BL_PATHS` for Austrian states (`"01"` through `"09"`) -- existing paths, unchanged
- `NEIGHBOR_PATHS` for countries (`"10"` through `"18"`) -- new paths

**Step 3.4: Create the SVG path generation script**

Create a one-time script `scripts/generate-neighbor-paths.mjs` that:

1. Downloads or reads GeoJSON for each country from Natural Earth (simplified 110m is sufficient)
2. Applies the projection: `svgX = (lon - 9.500931) / (17.163182 - 9.500931) * 612.94`, `svgY = (49.015279 - lat) / (49.015279 - 46.371323) * 313.55`
3. Converts polygon coordinates to SVG path `d` strings
4. Calculates centroid for label positions
5. Outputs a TypeScript constant that can be pasted into `AustriaMap.tsx`

Alternatively, the developer can use any GeoJSON-to-SVG tool and manually adjust coordinates. The key is matching the projection.

**Step 3.5: Update the viewBox**

Change the SVG viewBox from `"-5 -5 623 324"` to approximately `"-100 -100 900 550"` (exact values to be determined after generating the country paths).

Also update the `aria-label` to reflect the new scope:
```
aria-label="Karte von Österreich und Nachbarländern mit Teilnehmern"
```

---

#### PHASE 4: Update Other Frontend Components

**Step 4.1: Update LandMode.tsx BUNDESLAND_NAMES**

In `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`, line 5-16, extend the `BUNDESLAND_NAMES` map to include all international codes:

```typescript
const BUNDESLAND_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  '01': 'OE1 Wien',
  '02': 'OE2 Salzburg',
  // ... (existing)
  '09': 'OE9 Vorarlberg',
  '10': 'Slowenien',
  '11': 'Tschechien',
  '12': 'Deutschland',
  '13': 'Italien',
  '14': 'Slowakei',
  '15': 'Kroatien',
  '16': 'Liechtenstein',
  '17': 'Schweiz',
  '18': 'Ungarn',
};
```

Currently it only has `'10': 'Slowenien'`. This should be extended for completeness as more international repeaters may be added.

**Step 4.2: Update ExerciseSetupPage.tsx (optional)**

The BUNDESLAENDER constant in ExerciseSetupPage.tsx (lines 6-16) only lists Austrian states. If international repeaters should also be configurable through the exercise setup UI, this array should be extended. However, based on the current architecture where international repeaters already exist in the seed data, this may not be necessary for this feature. The international repeaters already appear under their bundesland_code grouping when added to an exercise.

---

#### PHASE 5: Testing and Edge Cases

**Step 5.1: Edge cases to handle**

- **OE callsigns with `bundesland_code` already set**: The migration should NOT overwrite existing values. Only fill in where `bundesland_code IS NULL`.
- **Unknown callsign prefixes**: The utility returns `null`. These operators remain with `bundesland_code = NULL`. The SQL should handle this gracefully (COALESCE to "Sonstige").
- **Portable callsigns**: A callsign like `OE3ABC/P` or `DL1ABC/OE8` with suffixes. The `callsignToCountryCode` function should strip suffixes (everything after `/`) before matching.
- **Special Austrian callsigns**: `OE0` prefix is sometimes used for special stations. The function should handle this (currently `OE0` would not match since the digit check is `>= '1'`). Consider mapping OE0 to a default or leaving it null.

**Step 5.2: Testing the callsign mapping**

Key test cases:
- `OE8YML` -> `"08"` (Kaernten)
- `S55UJE` -> `"10"` (Slowenien)
- `DL1ABC` -> `"12"` (Deutschland)
- `OK1ABC` -> `"11"` (Tschechien)
- `HB9XYZ` -> `"17"` (Schweiz)
- `HB0ABC` -> `"16"` (Liechtenstein)
- `9A1ABC` -> `"15"` (Kroatien)
- `IK2ABC` -> `"13"` (Italien)
- `OM3ABC` -> `"14"` (Slowakei)
- `HA5ABC` -> `"18"` (Ungarn)

---

### Implementation Order

1. **`server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts`** -- Create the utility function (no dependencies)
2. **`server/src/db/schema.ts`** -- Add the backfill migration (depends on #1)
3. **`server/src/routes/operators.ts`** -- Use utility in POST handler (depends on #1)
4. **`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** -- Simplify SQL queries (depends on #2 being run)
5. **`scripts/generate-neighbor-paths.mjs`** (or manual process) -- Generate SVG paths for neighbors
6. **`client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`** -- Add neighbor paths, change key scheme, expand viewBox
7. **`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** -- Fix the data code mapping
8. **`client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`** -- Extend BUNDESLAND_NAMES

Steps 1-4 are server-side and can be done as a unit. Steps 5-8 are frontend and can be done as a unit. Step 5 (path generation) can be done in parallel with 1-4.

---

### Potential Challenges

1. **SVG path generation**: This is the most labor-intensive step. The paths must align precisely with the existing Austrian Bundesland paths. If the projection formula does not perfectly match (e.g., if MapSVG used a slightly different projection), there may be visible gaps or overlaps at borders. Careful testing with a visual preview is needed.

2. **viewBox expansion**: Expanding the viewBox means Austria will appear proportionally smaller. This needs visual tuning to ensure the map remains readable, especially on mobile screens.

3. **Performance**: Adding 9 more country paths (which could be quite detailed) increases SVG complexity. Using simplified GeoJSON (110m from Natural Earth) mitigates this. If needed, paths can be simplified further using a tool like mapshaper.

4. **Liechtenstein**: It is extremely small geographically. Its SVG path might be barely visible. A special treatment (minimum size or icon marker) may be needed.

5. **Callsign suffix stripping**: Callsigns with `/P`, `/M`, `/OE8`, etc. need to be handled. The utility should strip everything after the first `/` before matching.

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts` - New file: core callsign-to-country mapping utility used by all other changes
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` - Major rewrite: add neighbor country SVG paths, change key scheme from "OE1"-"OE9" to "01"-"18", expand viewBox
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts` - Fix broken SQL: remove SUBSTR-based fallback in blStats and participants queries (lines 128-155)
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` - Fix data mapping on line 248: change from `OE${parseInt(...)}` to raw bundesland_code
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts` - Add migration: backfill bundesland_code for existing operators using the new utility function

     1→# Austria Map Feature — Bundesland Statistics
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→The ReportsPage already shows per-Bundesland statistics (participants, reports) in tables and charts. Adding a choropleth map of Austria colored by Bundesland gives an immediate visual overview — which regions are active, which are missing.
     5→
     6→## Approach
     7→Create an inline SVG component `AustriaMap` with the 9 Bundesländer shapes. No external map library needed — the shapes are static and simple. Color intensity based on participant count per Bundesland. Place it in ReportsPage between the summary cards and the charts.
     8→
     9→## Changes
    10→
    11→### 1. New component: `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`
    12→- Inline SVG with path data for all 9 Austrian Bundesländer (OE1–OE9)
    13→- Props: `data: { code: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]`
    14→- Color scale: white (0 participants) → light blue → dark blue (`#1e3a5f`) based on max value
    15→- Each Bundesland shape shows:
    16→  - Fill color based on participant count

    25→- Pass `blStats` data to the map component
    26→- Add ref + download button for PNG export (same pattern as existing charts)
    27→- Full-width card with title "Teilnehmer nach Bundesland"
    28→- Include color legend (gradient bar with min/max labels)
    29→
    30→## SVG Path Strategy
    31→Use simplified SVG paths for Austria's Bundesländer. The paths represent approximate geographic shapes positioned correctly relative to each other within a ~800×500 viewBox. Each path has an `id` matching the bundesland code ("01"–"09") for data binding.
    32→
    33→## Color Scale
    34→```
    35→0 participants  → #e8eef5 (very light blue-gray)

  "content": "# Nachbarländer auf der Karte + automatische Rufzeichen-Zuordnung\n\n## Context\nNachbarländer nehmen an BOS-ARSA-Übungen teil (z.B. S55UJE aus Slowenien). Die Karte zeigt derzeit nur Österreichs 9 Bundesländer. Ausländische Teilnehmer werden falsch zugeordnet (z.B. S55UJE → \"05\" OÖ statt \"10\" Slowenien), weil der Server `SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)` verwendet, was nur für OE-Rufzeichen funktioniert.\n\nZiel: Nachbarländer als Hintergrund auf der Karte zeigen, Statistiken erweitern, Rufzeichen automatisch korrekt zuordnen.\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Server: Callsign-to-Country Utility (NEW FILE)\n**`server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts`**\n\n```typescript\nexport function callsignToCountryCode(callsign: string): string | null\n```\n\nMapping (longest prefix first):\n- `OE1`-`OE9` → `\"01\"`-`\"09\"` (Austrian states)\n- `HB0` → `\"16\"` (Liechtenstein — before HB!)\n- `HB` → `\"17\"` (Schweiz)\n- `S5` → `\"10\"` (Slowenien)\n- `OK`/`OL` → `\"11\"` (Tschechien)\n- `D[A-R]` → `\"12\"` (Deutschland)\n- `I` → `\"13\"` (Italien)\n- `OM` → `\"14\"` (Slowakei)\n- `9A` → `\"15\"` (Kroatien)\n- `HA`/`HG` → `\"18\"` (Ungarn)\n\nStrip `/P`, `/M`, `/OE8` suffixes before matching.\n\n### 2. Server: Migration to backfill bundesland_code\n**`server/src/db/schema.ts`** — add after existing migrations\n\n- SELECT all operators WHERE `bundesland_code IS NULL`\n- For each, compute code via `callsignToCountryCode()` and UPDATE\n- Also used for OE callsigns that were stored with null bundesland_code\n\n### 3. Server: Use utility in operator creation\n**`server/src/routes/operators.ts`** — POST handler\n\n- If `bundesland_code` not provided, auto-derive from callsign\n- `const derivedBl = bundesland_code || callsignToCountryCode(callsign)`\n\n### 4. Server: Simplify stats SQL\n**`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — lines 128-155\n\nRemove broken `'0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)` fallback. After migration, all operators have `bundesland_code`, so queries simplify to:\n\n```sql\n-- blStats: just use o.bundesland_code directly\nLEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\nGROUP BY o.bundesland_code\n\n-- participants: remove the bl2 double-join hack\nLEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\n```\n\n### 5. Frontend: Neighbor country SVG paths\n**`client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`**\n\n- Use the known geo-projection: `svgX = (lon - 9.5009) / 7.6623 * 612.94`, `svgY = (49.0153 - lat) / 2.6440 * 313.55`\n- Get GeoJSON from Natural Earth (110m simplified), project to SVG, clip to expanded area\n- Hardcode resulting paths as `NEIGHBOR_PATHS` (codes `\"10\"`-`\"18\"`)\n- Expand viewBox from `\"-5 -5 623 324\"` to ~`\"-100 -100 900 550\"` to show neighbor borders\n- Render neighbors as background layer: light gray fill (`#e5e7eb`), thin gray stroke, 0.6 opacity\n- Countries with data: fill with blue scale (same as Austrian states)\n- Change internal key scheme from `\"OE1\"`-`\"OE9\"` to `\"01\"`-`\"09\"` (DB codes)\n- Add `BL_SHORT` map for display labels: `\"01\"→\"OE1\"`, `\"10\"→\"SI\"`, `\"12\"→\"DE\"`, etc.\n\n### 6. Frontend: Fix ReportsPage data mapping\n**`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** — line 248\n\nChange from:\n```tsx\ncode: `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}`\n```\nTo:\n```tsx\ncode: bl.bundesland_code\n```\n\nUpdate heading: \"Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land\"\n\n## Files Modified\n1. `server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts` (NEW)\n2. `server/src/db/schema.ts` (migration)\n3. `server/src/routes/operators.ts` (auto-derive bundesland_code)\n4. `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` (simplify SQL)\n5. `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` (add neighbors, change keys)\n6. `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (fix data mapping)\n\n## Verification\n1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"npm run build\"` compiles\n2. Server starts, migration backfills existing operators\n3. Creating operator with foreign callsign auto-assigns correct country\n4. ReportsPage shows neighbor countries on map with correct stats\n5. Austrian Bundesländer still display correctly\n6. Deploy to production via `bash deploy-production.sh`\n"
}
